TERVORO DIGITAL PUBLISHING — INSTALLED THIRD-PARTY NOTICES

Generated from the installed packages pinned by package-lock.json. Includes runtime and development dependencies present on this platform. Optional binaries for other platforms are not installed and are not included here.

This source project installs its dependencies through npm; it does not package a native application or redistribute node_modules. Independent HTML exports include a separate notice file for their actual bundled reader dependencies.

The page-flip 2.0.7 runtime bundles contain the application patch documented in docs/ENGINE_EVALUATION.md. Original license text is preserved below. Original fixture artwork and writing are described in fixtures/README.md.
